Methodology

1
AOIStructured area of interest geometry and area calculation.
2
JRC forest baselineJRC Global Forest Cover 2020 baseline within the AOI.
3
Hansen lossPost-2020 loss-year evidence filtered to the assessment period.
4
Optional commodity layerConfigured commodity evidence when available for this AOI.
5
Spatial intersectionDerived overlaps between AOI, baseline forest, loss, and commodity evidence.
6
Evidence-package generationCanonical JSON, HTML, PDF, metrics, and checksums generated for audit.

JRC-FDP Coffee Morphology And Threshold Sensitivity

Definitions: JRC forest 2020 is the baseline forest mask; FDP 2020 and 2024 probabilities are continuous coffee-model observations; current spatial overlap is JRC 2020 intersected with FDP coffee 2024; new-coffee overlap is JRC 2020 intersected with FDP 2024 pixels that were below the same threshold in 2020. The 0.025 views are permissive 2.5% FDP probability diagnostics, not verified plantation parcels.

At threshold 0.025 (a permissive 2.5% FDP probability diagnostic threshold), JRC 2020 intersecting FDP new coffee measures 17.25 ha; at 0.25 it measures 5.67 ha (-67.1% change). The t=0.025 new-overlap boundary-adjacency fraction is 0.54029, and median bounding-box elongation is 1.444444. The morphology is mixed or inconclusive from shape metrics alone.

Current FDP coffee mask nesting as threshold increases: true. New-coffee masks are not assumed to be nested.

jrc_nodata_inside_aoiJRC GFC2020 nodata pixels inside AOI were excluded from forest baseline metrics.
hansen_nodata_inside_aoiHansen lossyear nodata pixels inside AOI were excluded from loss metrics.
grid_alignment_transformation_appliedCategorical rasters were aligned to the deterministic target grid with nearest-neighbour resampling.
commodity_threshold_not_locally_calibratedThe configured probability threshold (0.025) is a generic model cutoff; it has not been locally calibrated for this AOI or country.
commodity_local_validation_missingNo locally validated (ground-truthed) accuracy assessment for the configured commodity probability model is supplied for Brazil by this run; localized accuracy assessment is the consumer's responsibility.
commodity_absence_not_establishedLow or missing localized commodity-probability evidence is not evidence that the commodity is absent: it reflects a missing localizing signal, not a negative finding.
